<div id="about"><div id="card">
<h2>Nobody sends you the sea.</h2>
<p>It is 9,216 cells obeying one rule, and your browser is computing it now. The server only keeps the order of things: once a second it sends what was done that tick, which is the touches people planted and a hash chained onto the ticks before it. Anyone with the same law and the same touches gets the same sea.</p>
<p>The server cannot see the sea, so the pages vouch for it. Every 60 ticks each one hashes its sea and tells the server; the first also sends the sea itself, which becomes a <b>fossil</b>, and the rest are counted as agreeing or not. The count is evidence: anonymous pages can lie.</p>
<p><b>Replay</b> a fossil and your browser takes the one before it and the touches in between, runs the law 60 times and compares its hash with the one kept. <b>Fork</b> starts a sea of your own from it.</p>
<p>The law is <a href="/law.js" target="_blank">/law.js</a>. This page runs the law whose sha256 is <code id="lawhash">...</code> and the server says the world's is <code id="lawhash2">...</code>.</p>
<p><button id="close">Back to the sea</button></p>
</div></div>
<script src="/law.js"></script>
<script>
const T = Tidewheel;
const { W, H, GENS, ERA, FOSSIL, LAWS } = T;
const TICK_MS = 1000;
const STOPS = [
  ["#040a1c", "#0a2349", "#12508a", "#2f9bb0", "#e9fbf0"],
  ["#10051f", "#3b0f4f", "#7a1b5e", "#c2325a", "#ef6a4a", "#ffb25b", "#ffe8a8", "#9fe0c0", "#3fb3c2", "#2a6fb0", "#2a3a8c", "#1a1650"],
];
const hex = (h) => [1, 3, 5].map((i) => parseInt(h.slice(i, i + 2), 16));
const PAL = LAWS.map((law, li) => {
  const st = STOPS[li].map(hex);
  return Array.from({ length: law.S }, (_, s) => {
    const p = (s / (law.S - 1)) * (st.length - 1);
    const a = Math.floor(p);
    const b = Math.min(a + 1, st.length - 1);
    const f = p - a;
    return st[a].map((v, k) => v + (st[b][k] - v) * f);
  });
});
const lawIndex = (n) => Math.floor(n / ERA) % LAWS.length;
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
// sha256 of a text, hex: the browser's, or a small one of our own where it has none (a page not
// served from localhost or https has no crypto.subtle).
const own = (text) => {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
  const primes = [];
  for (let c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every((p) => c % p)) primes.push(c);
  const frac = (x) => x - Math.floor(x);
  const K = primes.map((p) => Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296));
  const h = primes.slice(0, 8).map((p) => Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296));
  const size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6;
  const buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes);
  buf[bytes.length] = 0x80;
  const view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912));
  view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  const w = new Array(64);
  const rotr = (x, n) => (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n));
  for (let off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (let i = 16; i < 64; i++) {
      const s0 = rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3);
      const s1 = rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10);
      w[i] = (w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1) >>> 0;
    }
    let [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, hh] = h;
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      const t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      const t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach((v, i) => { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map((v) => v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")).join("");
};
const sha = async (text) => {
  if (!(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) return own(text);
  const d = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(d), (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
};
const colors = (grid, n) => {
  const pal = PAL[lawIndex(n)];
  const out = new Float32Array(W * H * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const c = pal[grid.charCodeAt(i) - 48] || pal[0];
    out[i * 3] = c[0]; out[i * 3 + 1] = c[1]; out[i * 3 + 2] = c[2];
  }
  return out;
};
const paint = (cv, rgb) => {
  const ctx = cv.getContext("2d");
  const img = ctx.createImageData(W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    img.data[i * 4] = rgb[i * 3]; img.data[i * 4 + 1] = rgb[i * 3 + 1]; img.data[i * 4 + 2] = rgb[i * 3 + 2]; img.data[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
};
// What is drawn: the world's sea (live), a fossil (frozen), or a sea of one's own (fork).
let mode = "live", shown = null, fork = null, forkTimer = 0;
let from = null, to = null, since = 0;
let current = null;
const show = (grid, n, soft) => {
  const next = colors(grid, n);
  from = soft && current ? current : next;
  to = next;
  since = performance.now();
};
const frame = (t) => {
  if (to) {
    const e = Math.min(1, (t - since) / (TICK_MS - 20));
    const k = e * e * (3 - 2 * e);
    current = to.map((v, i) => from[i] + (v - from[i]) * k);
    paint($("sea"), current);
    paint($("glow"), current);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
};
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
const clock = (s) => Math.floor(s / 60) + ":" + String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
const setSync = (kind, text) => { $("dot").className = kind; $("sync").textContent = text; };
// The world's sea, as this page has computed it: its tick, its hash, its cells.
let sea = null, chain = "", seaBase = "";
const hud = () => {
  const n = mode === "fork" ? fork.n : mode === "fossil" ? shown.n : sea ? sea.n : 0;
  $("tick").textContent = n.toLocaleString();
  $("era").textContent = LAWS[lawIndex(n)].name;
  $("turns").textContent = clock(Math.round(((ERA - (n % ERA)) * TICK_MS) / 1000));
  if (chain && mode !== "fork") $("chainhash").textContent = chain.slice(0, 8);
};
const groupBy = (items) => {
  const out = {};
  Object.keys(items || {}).sort().forEach((k) => { (out[Number(k.slice(0, 8))] = out[Number(k.slice(0, 8))] || []).push(items[k]); });
  return out;
};
let queue = [], joining = false, lawText = "", lawHash = "";
const join = async () => {
  joining = true;
  setSync("", "joining");
  const sync = await (await fetch("/sync")).json();
  lawText = lawText || (await (await fetch("/law.js")).text());
  lawHash = await sha(lawText);
  seaBase = await sha("sea|" + sync.law + "|" + sync.seed);
  let n = 0, grid = null, h = seaBase, ch = sync.chain0;
  if (sync.fossil.n > 0) { n = sync.fossil.n; grid = sync.fossil.grid; h = sync.fossil.h; ch = sync.fossil.chain; } else { grid = T.genesis(sync.seed); }
  const byTick = groupBy(sync.inputs);
  for (let t = n + 1; t <= sync.n; t++) {
    const ts = byTick[t] || [];
    ch = await sha(ch + "|" + t + "|" + JSON.stringify(ts));
    grid = T.step(grid, ts, t, ch);
    h = await sha(h + "|" + t + "|" + grid);
    if (t % 25 === 0) setSync("", "catching up, " + (sync.n - t).toLocaleString() + " ticks to go");
  }
  sea = { n: sync.n, h, grid };
  chain = ch;
  joining = false;
  const sound = lawHash === sync.law && ch === sync.h;
  setSync(sound ? "in" : "out", sound ? "in the world" : lawHash !== sync.law ? "this is not the world's law" : "the touches do not add up to the chain");
  if (mode === "live") show(sea.grid, sea.n, false);
  hud();
  const waiting = queue; queue = [];
  waiting.forEach(onPack);
  loadFossils();
};
// A join that fails (the server restarting, say) is tried again, the packs waiting meanwhile.
const rejoin = () => join().catch(() => { joining = false; setSync("", "retrying"); setTimeout(rejoin, 2000); });
let steps = Promise.resolve();
const onPack = (pack) => {
  steps = steps.then(async () => {
    if (joining || !sea) { queue.push(pack); return; }
    if (pack.n <= sea.n) return;
    if (pack.n !== sea.n + 1) { await rejoin(); return; }
    const expect = await sha(chain + "|" + pack.n + "|" + JSON.stringify(pack.t));
    chain = pack.h;
    const grid = T.step(sea.grid, pack.t, pack.n, pack.h);
    const h = await sha(sea.h + "|" + pack.n + "|" + grid);
    sea = { n: pack.n, h, grid };
    pending = pending.filter((r) => r.tick > pack.n || (r.el.remove(), false));
    if (expect !== pack.h) setSync("out", "the server's chain broke at " + pack.n);
    if (mode === "live") show(grid, pack.n, true);
    hud();
    if (pack.n % FOSSIL === 0) witness(sea);
  });
};
// Vouch for the sea at a fossil tick: the hash first, and the sea itself if nobody has sent it.
const id = (() => { try { return sessionStorage.tidewheel || (sessionStorage.tidewheel = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 14)); } catch (e) { return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 14); } })();
const witness = async (s) => {
  const post = (body) => fetch("/witness", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(body) }).then((r) => r.json());
  let said = await post({ id, n: s.n, h: s.h });
  if (said.need) said = await post({ id, n: s.n, h: s.h, grid: s.grid });
  const at = "at #" + s.n.toLocaleString();
  $("agreed").innerHTML = !said.mine ? '<b class="bad">' + at + ", your sea is not the fossil's</b>" : said.dissent ? at + " <b>" + said.agree + "</b> agree, <b class=bad>" + said.dissent + "</b> disagree" : at + " <b>" + said.agree + "</b> other " + (said.agree === 1 ? "browser agrees" : "browsers agree");
  setTimeout(loadFossils, 600);
};
const open = () => {
  const es = new EventSource("/live");
  es.onmessage = (e) => onPack(JSON.parse(e.data));
  es.onerror = () => setSync("", "reconnecting");
  es.onopen = () => { if (!sea) rejoin(); };
};
// Touches: kept for two ticks from now. A ring marks one until its tick has come.
let pending = [], lastTouch = 0, down = false;
const ring = (x, y, ms) => {
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  el.className = "ring";
  el.style.left = ((x + 0.5) / W) * 100 + "%";
  el.style.top = ((y + 0.5) / H) * 100 + "%";
  $("rings").append(el);
  if (ms) setTimeout(() => el.remove(), ms);
  return el;
};
const plant = async (ev) => {
  const r = $("stage").getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = Math.floor(((ev.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * W);
  const y = Math.floor(((ev.clientY - r.top) / r.height) * H);
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= W || y >= H) return;
  $("hint").style.opacity = 0;
  if (mode === "fork") { fork.touches.push({ x, y }); ring(x, y, 1200); return; }
  if (mode !== "live") return;
  const t = performance.now();
  if (t - lastTouch < 240) return;
  lastTouch = t;
  const res = await fetch("/touch", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ x, y }) });
  if (res.ok) { const k = await res.json(); pending.push({ tick: k.tick, el: ring(x, y, 0) }); }
};
$("stage").addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => { down = true; plant(e); });
$("stage").addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => { if (down) plant(e); });
addEventListener("pointerup", () => { down = false; });
// Fossils.
const cache = new Map();
const fossil = async (n) => {
  if (!cache.has(n)) {
    const res = await fetch("/fossil/" + n);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    cache.set(n, await res.json());
  }
  return cache.get(n);
};
const loadFossils = async () => {
  const list = await (await fetch("/fossils")).json();
  const box = $("fossils");
  for (const f of list) {
    const old = box.querySelector('[data-n="' + f.n + '"]');
    if (old) continue;
    const full = await fossil(f.n);
    if (!full) continue;
    const b = document.createElement("button");
    b.className = "fossil"; b.dataset.n = f.n;
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas"); cv.width = W; cv.height = H;
    paint(cv, colors(full.grid, f.n));
    const label = document.createElement("small"); label.textContent = "#" + f.n.toLocaleString();
    b.append(cv, label);
    b.onclick = () => openFossil(f.n);
    box.insertBefore(b, box.firstChild);
  }
};
const banner = (html) => { $("banner").innerHTML = html; $("banner").classList.toggle("on", !!html); };
const openFossil = async (n) => {
  clearInterval(forkTimer);
  mode = "fossil";
  cache.delete(n);
  shown = await fossil(n);
  document.querySelectorAll(".fossil").forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle("on", Number(b.dataset.n) === n));
  show(shown.grid, shown.n, true);
  hud();
  banner('<span>Fossil <b>#' + n.toLocaleString() + '</b>, kept ' + new Date(shown.at * 1000).toLocaleTimeString() + ', ' + shown.agree + ' agree' + (shown.dissent ? ', ' + shown.dissent + ' disagree' : '') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><span id="verdict"></span><button id="replay">Replay</button><button id="forkit">Fork</button><button id="back">Back to the sea</button>');
  $("replay").onclick = replay;
  $("forkit").onclick = () => startFork(shown);
  $("back").onclick = backToLive;
};
const backToLive = () => {
  clearInterval(forkTimer);
  mode = "live"; fork = null;
  document.querySelectorAll(".fossil").forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("on"));
  banner("");
  if (sea) show(sea.grid, sea.n, true);
  hud();
};
const replay = async () => {
  const v = $("verdict");
  v.className = ""; v.textContent = "replaying...";
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const before = await fossil(shown.n - FOSSIL);
  if (!before) { v.className = "bad"; v.textContent = "the fossil before it is not kept"; return; }
  const log = await (await fetch("/replay?from=" + before.n + "&to=" + shown.n)).json();
  const byTick = groupBy(log);
  let grid = before.grid, h = before.h, ch = before.chain, planted = 0;
  for (let t = before.n + 1; t <= shown.n; t++) {
    const ts = byTick[t] || [];
    planted += ts.length;
    ch = await sha(ch + "|" + t + "|" + JSON.stringify(ts));
    grid = T.step(grid, ts, t, ch);
    h = await sha(h + "|" + t + "|" + grid);
  }
  const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
  if (h === shown.h && grid === shown.grid && ch === shown.chain) { v.className = "ok"; v.textContent = "Same sea: " + FOSSIL + " ticks and " + planted + " touches replayed here in " + ms + " ms; the sea's hash " + h.slice(0, 10) + "... and the server's chain " + ch.slice(0, 10) + "... both match."; }
  else { v.className = "bad"; v.textContent = "Not the same: replayed " + h.slice(0, 10) + "... (chain " + ch.slice(0, 10) + "...), the world kept " + shown.h.slice(0, 10) + "... (chain " + shown.chain.slice(0, 10) + "...)"; }
};
const startFork = (f) => {
  mode = "fork";
  fork = { n: f.n, grid: f.grid, touches: [] };
  banner('<span>Your own sea, forked at <b>#' + f.n.toLocaleString() + '</b>. Press it to plant whirls: nothing here reaches the world.</span><span class="sp"></span><button id="back">Back to the sea</button>');
  $("back").onclick = backToLive;
  clearInterval(forkTimer);
  forkTimer = setInterval(() => {
    fork.n += 1;
    fork.grid = T.step(fork.grid, fork.touches, fork.n, ((Math.imul(fork.n, 2654435761) >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")));
    fork.touches = [];
    show(fork.grid, fork.n, true);
    hud();
  }, TICK_MS / 2);
};
$("why").onclick = async () => {
  $("about").classList.add("on");
  $("lawhash").textContent = lawHash.slice(0, 12) + "...";
  const sync = await (await fetch("/sync")).json();
  $("lawhash2").textContent = sync.law.slice(0, 12) + "...";
};
$("close").onclick = () => $("about").classList.remove("on");
// The blur that melts the cells into waves grows with the cells' size on screen.
const fit = () => { $("sea").style.filter = "blur(" + (($("stage").getBoundingClientRect().width / W) * 0.3).toFixed(2) + "px) saturate(1.25) contrast(1.08)"; };
addEventListener("resize", fit);
fit();
open();
setInterval(hud, 1000);
setTimeout(() => { $("hint").style.opacity = 0; }, 15000);
</script>

<div id="about"><div id="card">
<h2>Nobody sends you the sea.</h2>
<p>It is 9,216 cells obeying one rule, and your browser is computing it now. The server only keeps the order of things: once a second it sends what was done that tick, which is the touches people planted and a hash chained onto the ticks before it. Anyone with the same law and the same touches gets the same sea.</p>
<p>The server cannot see the sea, so the pages vouch for it. Every 60 ticks each one hashes its sea and tells the server; the first also sends the sea itself, which becomes a <b>fossil</b>, and the rest are counted as agreeing or not. The count is evidence: anonymous pages can lie.</p>
<p><b>Replay</b> a fossil and your browser takes the one before it and the touches in between, runs the law 60 times and compares its hash with the one kept. <b>Fork</b> starts a sea of your own from it.</p>
<p>The law is <a href="/law.js" target="_blank">/law.js</a>. This page runs the law whose sha256 is <code id="lawhash">...</code> and the server says the world's is <code id="lawhash2">...</code>.</p>
<p><button id="close">Back to the sea</button></p>
</div></div>
<script src="/law.js"></script>
<script>
const T = Tidewheel;
const { W, H, GENS, ERA, FOSSIL, LAWS } = T;
const TICK_MS = 1000;
const STOPS = [
  ["#040a1c", "#0a2349", "#12508a", "#2f9bb0", "#e9fbf0"],
  ["#10051f", "#3b0f4f", "#7a1b5e", "#c2325a", "#ef6a4a", "#ffb25b", "#ffe8a8", "#9fe0c0", "#3fb3c2", "#2a6fb0", "#2a3a8c", "#1a1650"],
];
const hex = (h) => [1, 3, 5].map((i) => parseInt(h.slice(i, i + 2), 16));
const PAL = LAWS.map((law, li) => {
  const st = STOPS[li].map(hex);
  return Array.from({ length: law.S }, (_, s) => {
    const p = (s / (law.S - 1)) * (st.length - 1);
    const a = Math.floor(p);
    const b = Math.min(a + 1, st.length - 1);
    const f = p - a;
    return st[a].map((v, k) => v + (st[b][k] - v) * f);
  });
});
const lawIndex = (n) => Math.floor(n / ERA) % LAWS.length;
const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
// sha256 of a text, hex: the browser's, or a small one of our own where it has none (a page not
// served from localhost or https has no crypto.subtle).
const own = (text) => {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
  const primes = [];
  for (let c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every((p) => c % p)) primes.push(c);
  const frac = (x) => x - Math.floor(x);
  const K = primes.map((p) => Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296));
  const h = primes.slice(0, 8).map((p) => Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296));
  const size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6;
  const buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes);
  buf[bytes.length] = 0x80;
  const view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912));
  view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  const w = new Array(64);
  const rotr = (x, n) => (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n));
  for (let off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (let i = 16; i < 64; i++) {
      const s0 = rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3);
      const s1 = rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10);
      w[i] = (w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1) >>> 0;
    }
    let [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, hh] = h;
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      const t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      const t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = c; c = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach((v, i) => { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map((v) => v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")).join("");
};
const sha = async (text) => {
  if (!(window.crypto && crypto.subtle)) return own(text);
  const d = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(d), (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
};
const colors = (grid, n) => {
  const pal = PAL[lawIndex(n)];
  const out = new Float32Array(W * H * 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    const c = pal[grid.charCodeAt(i) - 48] || pal[0];
    out[i * 3] = c[0]; out[i * 3 + 1] = c[1]; out[i * 3 + 2] = c[2];
  }
  return out;
};
const paint = (cv, rgb) => {
  const ctx = cv.getContext("2d");
  const img = ctx.createImageData(W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < W * H; i++) {
    img.data[i * 4] = rgb[i * 3]; img.data[i * 4 + 1] = rgb[i * 3 + 1]; img.data[i * 4 + 2] = rgb[i * 3 + 2]; img.data[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
};
// What is drawn: the world's sea (live), a fossil (frozen), or a sea of one's own (fork).
let mode = "live", shown = null, fork = null, forkTimer = 0;
let from = null, to = null, since = 0;
let current = null;
const show = (grid, n, soft) => {
  const next = colors(grid, n);
  from = soft && current ? current : next;
  to = next;
  since = performance.now();
};
const frame = (t) => {
  if (to) {
    const e = Math.min(1, (t - since) / (TICK_MS - 20));
    const k = e * e * (3 - 2 * e);
    current = to.map((v, i) => from[i] + (v - from[i]) * k);
    paint($("sea"), current);
    paint($("glow"), current);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
};
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
const clock = (s) => Math.floor(s / 60) + ":" + String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
const setSync = (kind, text) => { $("dot").className = kind; $("sync").textContent = text; };
// The world's sea, as this page has computed it: its tick, its hash, its cells.
let sea = null, chain = "", seaBase = "";
const hud = () => {
  const n = mode === "fork" ? fork.n : mode === "fossil" ? shown.n : sea ? sea.n : 0;
  $("tick").textContent = n.toLocaleString();
  $("era").textContent = LAWS[lawIndex(n)].name;
  $("turns").textContent = clock(Math.round(((ERA - (n % ERA)) * TICK_MS) / 1000));
  if (chain && mode !== "fork") $("chainhash").textContent = chain.slice(0, 8);
};
const groupBy = (items) => {
  const out = {};
  Object.keys(items || {}).sort().forEach((k) => { (out[Number(k.slice(0, 8))] = out[Number(k.slice(0, 8))] || []).push(items[k]); });
  return out;
};
let queue = [], joining = false, lawText = "", lawHash = "";
const join = async () => {
  joining = true;
  setSync("", "joining");
  const sync = await (await fetch("/sync")).json();
  lawText = lawText || (await (await fetch("/law.js")).text());
  lawHash = await sha(lawText);
  seaBase = await sha("sea|" + sync.law + "|" + sync.seed);
  let n = 0, grid = null, h = seaBase, ch = sync.chain0;
  if (sync.fossil.n > 0) { n = sync.fossil.n; grid = sync.fossil.grid; h = sync.fossil.h; ch = sync.fossil.chain; } else { grid = T.genesis(sync.seed); }
  const byTick = groupBy(sync.inputs);
  for (let t = n + 1; t <= sync.n; t++) {
    const ts = byTick[t] || [];
    ch = await sha(ch + "|" + t + "|" + JSON.stringify(ts));
    grid = T.step(grid, ts, t, ch);
    h = await sha(h + "|" + t + "|" + grid);
    if (t % 25 === 0) setSync("", "catching up, " + (sync.n - t).toLocaleString() + " ticks to go");
  }
  sea = { n: sync.n, h, grid };
  chain = ch;
  joining = false;
  const sound = lawHash === sync.law && ch === sync.h;
  setSync(sound ? "in" : "out", sound ? "in the world" : lawHash !== sync.law ? "this is not the world's law" : "the touches do not add up to the chain");
  if (mode === "live") show(sea.grid, sea.n, false);
  hud();
  const waiting = queue; queue = [];
  waiting.forEach(onPack);
  loadFossils();
};
// A join that fails (the server restarting, say) is tried again, the packs waiting meanwhile.
const rejoin = () => join().catch(() => { joining = false; setSync("", "retrying"); setTimeout(rejoin, 2000); });
let steps = Promise.resolve();
const onPack = (pack) => {
  steps = steps.then(async () => {
    if (joining || !sea) { queue.push(pack); return; }
    if (pack.n <= sea.n) return;
    if (pack.n !== sea.n + 1) { await rejoin(); return; }
    const expect = await sha(chain + "|" + pack.n + "|" + JSON.stringify(pack.t));
    chain = pack.h;
    const grid = T.step(sea.grid, pack.t, pack.n, pack.h);
    const h = await sha(sea.h + "|" + pack.n + "|" + grid);
    sea = { n: pack.n, h, grid };
    pending = pending.filter((r) => r.tick > pack.n || (r.el.remove(), false));
    if (expect !== pack.h) setSync("out", "the server's chain broke at " + pack.n);
    if (mode === "live") show(grid, pack.n, true);
    hud();
    if (pack.n % FOSSIL === 0) witness(sea);
  });
};
// Vouch for the sea at a fossil tick: the hash first, and the sea itself if nobody has sent it.
const id = (() => { try { return sessionStorage.tidewheel || (sessionStorage.tidewheel = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 14)); } catch (e) { return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 14); } })();
const witness = async (s) => {
  const post = (body) => fetch("/witness", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify(body) }).then((r) => r.json());
  let said = await post({ id, n: s.n, h: s.h });
  if (said.need) said = await post({ id, n: s.n, h: s.h, grid: s.grid });
  const at = "at #" + s.n.toLocaleString();
  $("agreed").innerHTML = !said.mine ? '<b class="bad">' + at + ", your sea is not the fossil's</b>" : said.dissent ? at + " <b>" + said.agree + "</b> agree, <b class=bad>" + said.dissent + "</b> disagree" : at + " <b>" + said.agree + "</b> other " + (said.agree === 1 ? "browser agrees" : "browsers agree");
  setTimeout(loadFossils, 600);
};
const open = () => {
  const es = new EventSource("/live");
  es.onmessage = (e) => onPack(JSON.parse(e.data));
  es.onerror = () => setSync("", "reconnecting");
  es.onopen = () => { if (!sea) rejoin(); };
};
// Touches: kept for two ticks from now. A ring marks one until its tick has come.
let pending = [], lastTouch = 0, down = false;
const ring = (x, y, ms) => {
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  el.className = "ring";
  el.style.left = ((x + 0.5) / W) * 100 + "%";
  el.style.top = ((y + 0.5) / H) * 100 + "%";
  $("rings").append(el);
  if (ms) setTimeout(() => el.remove(), ms);
  return el;
};
const plant = async (ev) => {
  const r = $("stage").getBoundingClientRect();
  const x = Math.floor(((ev.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * W);
  const y = Math.floor(((ev.clientY - r.top) / r.height) * H);
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= W || y >= H) return;
  $("hint").style.opacity = 0;
  if (mode === "fork") { fork.touches.push({ x, y }); ring(x, y, 1200); return; }
  if (mode !== "live") return;
  const t = performance.now();
  if (t - lastTouch < 240) return;
  lastTouch = t;
  const res = await fetch("/touch", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ x, y }) });
  if (res.ok) { const k = await res.json(); pending.push({ tick: k.tick, el: ring(x, y, 0) }); }
};
$("stage").addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => { down = true; plant(e); });
$("stage").addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => { if (down) plant(e); });
addEventListener("pointerup", () => { down = false; });
// Fossils.
const cache = new Map();
const fossil = async (n) => {
  if (!cache.has(n)) {
    const res = await fetch("/fossil/" + n);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    cache.set(n, await res.json());
  }
  return cache.get(n);
};
const loadFossils = async () => {
  const list = await (await fetch("/fossils")).json();
  const box = $("fossils");
  for (const f of list) {
    const old = box.querySelector('[data-n="' + f.n + '"]');
    if (old) continue;
    const full = await fossil(f.n);
    if (!full) continue;
    const b = document.createElement("button");
    b.className = "fossil"; b.dataset.n = f.n;
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas"); cv.width = W; cv.height = H;
    paint(cv, colors(full.grid, f.n));
    const label = document.createElement("small"); label.textContent = "#" + f.n.toLocaleString();
    b.append(cv, label);
    b.onclick = () => openFossil(f.n);
    box.insertBefore(b, box.firstChild);
  }
};
const banner = (html) => { $("banner").innerHTML = html; $("banner").classList.toggle("on", !!html); };
const openFossil = async (n) => {
  clearInterval(forkTimer);
  mode = "fossil";
  cache.delete(n);
  shown = await fossil(n);
  document.querySelectorAll(".fossil").forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle("on", Number(b.dataset.n) === n));
  show(shown.grid, shown.n, true);
  hud();
  banner('<span>Fossil <b>#' + n.toLocaleString() + '</b>, kept ' + new Date(shown.at * 1000).toLocaleTimeString() + ', ' + shown.agree + ' agree' + (shown.dissent ? ', ' + shown.dissent + ' disagree' : '') + '</span><span class="sp"></span><span id="verdict"></span><button id="replay">Replay</button><button id="forkit">Fork</button><button id="back">Back to the sea</button>');
  $("replay").onclick = replay;
  $("forkit").onclick = () => startFork(shown);
  $("back").onclick = backToLive;
};
const backToLive = () => {
  clearInterval(forkTimer);
  mode = "live"; fork = null;
  document.querySelectorAll(".fossil").forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("on"));
  banner("");
  if (sea) show(sea.grid, sea.n, true);
  hud();
};
const replay = async () => {
  const v = $("verdict");
  v.className = ""; v.textContent = "replaying...";
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const before = await fossil(shown.n - FOSSIL);
  if (!before) { v.className = "bad"; v.textContent = "the fossil before it is not kept"; return; }
  const log = await (await fetch("/replay?from=" + before.n + "&to=" + shown.n)).json();
  const byTick = groupBy(log);
  let grid = before.grid, h = before.h, ch = before.chain, planted = 0;
  for (let t = before.n + 1; t <= shown.n; t++) {
    const ts = byTick[t] || [];
    planted += ts.length;
    ch = await sha(ch + "|" + t + "|" + JSON.stringify(ts));
    grid = T.step(grid, ts, t, ch);
    h = await sha(h + "|" + t + "|" + grid);
  }
  const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
  if (h === shown.h && grid === shown.grid && ch === shown.chain) { v.className = "ok"; v.textContent = "Same sea: " + FOSSIL + " ticks and " + planted + " touches replayed here in " + ms + " ms; the sea's hash " + h.slice(0, 10) + "... and the server's chain " + ch.slice(0, 10) + "... both match."; }
  else { v.className = "bad"; v.textContent = "Not the same: replayed " + h.slice(0, 10) + "... (chain " + ch.slice(0, 10) + "...), the world kept " + shown.h.slice(0, 10) + "... (chain " + shown.chain.slice(0, 10) + "...)"; }
};
const startFork = (f) => {
  mode = "fork";
  fork = { n: f.n, grid: f.grid, touches: [] };
  banner('<span>Your own sea, forked at <b>#' + f.n.toLocaleString() + '</b>. Press it to plant whirls: nothing here reaches the world.</span><span class="sp"></span><button id="back">Back to the sea</button>');
  $("back").onclick = backToLive;
  clearInterval(forkTimer);
  forkTimer = setInterval(() => {
    fork.n += 1;
    fork.grid = T.step(fork.grid, fork.touches, fork.n, ((Math.imul(fork.n, 2654435761) >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")));
    fork.touches = [];
    show(fork.grid, fork.n, true);
    hud();
  }, TICK_MS / 2);
};
$("why").onclick = async () => {
  $("about").classList.add("on");
  $("lawhash").textContent = lawHash.slice(0, 12) + "...";
  const sync = await (await fetch("/sync")).json();
  $("lawhash2").textContent = sync.law.slice(0, 12) + "...";
};
$("close").onclick = () => $("about").classList.remove("on");
// The blur that melts the cells into waves grows with the cells' size on screen.
const fit = () => { $("sea").style.filter = "blur(" + (($("stage").getBoundingClientRect().width / W) * 0.3).toFixed(2) + "px) saturate(1.25) contrast(1.08)"; };
addEventListener("resize", fit);
fit();

setInterval(hud, 1000);
setTimeout(() => { $("hint").style.opacity = 0; }, 15000);

<script>
var FLEET = [4, 3, 3, 2];
var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
var memory = {};
var load = function (id) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("sea-battle:" + id)) || memory[id] || null; } catch (e) { return memory[id] || null; } };
var save = function (id, v) { memory[id] = v; try { localStorage.setItem("sea-battle:" + id, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} };

var hex = function (bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, function (b) { return (b + 256).toString(16).slice(1); }).join(""); };
var sha = function (text) {
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text)).then(function (d) { return hex(new Uint8Array(d)); });
  return Promise.resolve(own(text));
};
// sha256 for a page served over plain http on a network, where the browser offers none.
var own = function (text) {
  var bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text), primes = [];
  for (var c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every(function (p) { return c % p; })) primes.push(c);
  var frac = function (x) { return x - Math.floor(x); };
  var K = primes.map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var h = primes.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6, buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes); buf[bytes.length] = 128;
  var view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912)); view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  var w = new Array(64), rotr = function (x, n) { return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n)); };
  for (var off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (var i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++) w[i] = (w[i - 16] + (rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)) + w[i - 7] + (rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10))) >>> 0;
    var a = h[0], b = h[1], cc = h[2], d = h[3], e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      var t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      var t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = cc; cc = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, cc, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach(function (v, i) { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map(function (v) { return (v + 4294967296).toString(16).slice(1); }).join("");
};

// A fleet placed at random: ships 1 to 4, each a straight line, none overlapping.
var shuffle = function () {
  var cells = new Array(64).fill("0");
  FLEET.forEach(function (len, i) {
    for (;;) {
      var across = Math.random() < 0.5;
      var r = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 8 : 9 - len)), c = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 9 - len : 8));
      var at = [];
      for (var k = 0; k < len; k++) at.push((r + (across ? 0 : k)) * 8 + c + (across ? k : 0));
      if (at.every(function (x) { return cells[x] === "0"; })) { at.forEach(function (x) { cells[x] = String(i + 1); }); return; }
    }
  });
  return cells.join("");
};
var salt = function () { var b = new Uint8Array(16); crypto.getRandomValues(b); return hex(b); };

var grids = {};
var makeGrid = function (id, onClick) {
  var el = $(id), cells = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < 64; k++) {
    var btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label", "ABCDEFGH"[k % 8] + (Math.floor(k / 8) + 1));
    (function (k) { btn.onclick = function () { if (onClick) onClick(k); }; })(k);
    el.appendChild(btn); cells.push(btn);
  }
  grids[id] = cells;
};
var paint = function (id, cls) { grids[id].forEach(function (b, k) { b.className = cls(k); }); };

var post = function (path, body) {
  return fetch(path, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })
    .then(function (r) { return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (j) { return { ok: r.ok, body: j }; }); });
};

var gameId = location.hash.slice(1), game = null, mine = null, draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }, busy = false;

var marks = function (shots, k) {
  var s = shots.find(function (s) { return s.cell === k; });
  return s ? (s.said === "" ? " wait" : " " + s.said) : "";
};
var render = function () {
  var g = game;
  if (!g) {
    paint("left", function (k) { return draft.board[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : ""; });
    paint("right", function () { return ""; });
    $("leftname").textContent = "Your fleet (only you can see it)";
    $("rightname").textContent = "Their waters";
    return;
  }
  var me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var left = me || "a", right = me ? them : "b";
  var atLeft = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by !== left; });
  var atRight = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === left; });
  var boardOf = function (seat) { return seat === me ? mine.board : g[seat].board; };
  var lies = function (seat) { return g[seat].lies || []; };
  var cls = function (seat, shots) {
    return function (k) {
      var b = boardOf(seat);
      return (b && b[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : "") + marks(shots, k) + (lies(seat).indexOf(k) >= 0 ? " lie" : "");
    };
  };
  paint("left", cls(left, atLeft));
  paint("right", cls(right, atRight));
  $("leftname").textContent = (me ? "Your waters" : (g.a.name || "Captain A") + "'s waters");
  $("rightname").textContent = (g[right].name || "Waiting for a captain") + "'s waters";
  $("leftseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[left].commit || "...");
  $("rightseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[right].commit || "...");
  var aim = me && g.phase === "play" && g.turn === me && !g.shots.some(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  $("right").className = "grid" + (aim ? " aim" : "");
  $("lobby").style.display = "none";
  $("share").style.display = me === "a" && g.phase === "waiting" ? "" : "none";
  $("link").textContent = location.href;
  $("cheat").style.display = me && (g.phase === "play" || g.phase === "waiting") ? "" : "none";
  var hits = function (seat) { return g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === seat && s.said === "hit"; }).length; };
  var say = g.phase === "waiting" ? (me ? "Waiting for an opponent to join." : "This game is waiting for a second captain.")
    : g.phase === "play" ? (me ? (g.turn === me ? "Your turn: fire at their waters." : "Their turn.") : g[g.turn].name + " is firing.") + " Hits " + hits(left) + " / 12, taken " + hits(right) + " / 12."
    : g.phase === "reveal" ? "The battle is over. The boards are being revealed and checked."
    : g.winner ? g[g.winner].name + " wins." : "Nobody wins.";
  $("status").textContent = say;
  var v = $("verdict");
  if (g.phase === "over") {
    var line = function (seat) {
      var p = g[seat];
      if (!p.revealed) return "<p><b></b>: <span class=bad>went silent and never revealed a board</span></p>";
      return "<p><b></b>: " + (p.sealed ? "<span class=ok>board matches its seal</span>" : "<span class=bad>board does not match its seal</span>")
        + ", " + (p.fleet === "" ? "<span class=ok>legal fleet</span>" : "<span class=bad>illegal fleet</span>")
        + ", " + (p.lies.length ? "<span class=bad>" + p.lies.length + " false answers (marked in yellow)</span>" : "<span class=ok>every answer true</span>") + "</p>";
    };
    v.innerHTML = "<p id=why></p>" + line("a") + line("b");
    v.querySelector("#why").textContent = g.why;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[0].textContent = g.a.name;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[1].textContent = g.b.name;
    v.className = "on";
  } else v.className = "";
  silence();
  act();
};

// What this page does by itself: answer a shot at it, and reveal its board when the battle ends.
var act = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy) return;
  var waiting = g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === "" && s.by !== me; });
  if (g.phase === "play" && waiting) {
    var hit = mine.board[waiting.cell] !== "0";
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/answer", { key: mine.key, said: hit && !$("lie").checked ? "hit" : "miss" }).then(done);
  } else if (g.phase === "reveal" && !g[me].revealed) {
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/reveal", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
  }
};
var done = function (r) { busy = false; if (r.ok && r.body.id) { game = r.body; render(); } };

// When the opponent has kept everyone waiting two minutes, a button to claim the game.
var silence = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var pending = g && g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  var waitingOn = !g ? "" : g.phase === "reveal" ? (g[them].revealed ? "" : them) : g.phase !== "play" ? "" : pending ? (pending.by === me ? them : me) : g.turn;
  var left = g ? 120 - (Date.now() / 1000 - g.since) : 0;
  var can = me && waitingOn === them && (g.phase === "play" || g[me].revealed) && left <= 0;
  $("claimbar").style.display = can ? "" : "none";
  if (can) $("claim").textContent = g[them].name + " has gone silent. Claim the game";
};
setInterval(function () { if (game) silence(); }, 5000);
var claim = function () {
  if (busy) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/claim", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
};

var fire = function (k) {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy || g.phase !== "play" || g.turn !== me) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/fire", { key: mine.key, cell: k }).then(done);
};

var refresh = function () {
  return fetch("/games/" + gameId).then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; }).then(function (g) {
    game = g;
    if (!g) { $("status").textContent = "There is no game " + gameId + "."; return; }
    if (!mine && g.phase === "waiting") {
      $("start").textContent = "Join " + (g.a.name || "this game");
      $("lobby").style.display = "";
      $("status").textContent = (g.a.name || "A captain") + " is waiting for an opponent. Shuffle your fleet and join.";
      game = null; render(); game = g;
      return;
    }
    render();
  });
};

var begin = function () {
  var name = $("name").value.trim();
  $("start").disabled = true;
  sha(draft.board + "|" + draft.salt).then(function (commit) {
    return post(gameId ? "/games/" + gameId + "/join" : "/games", { name: name || undefined, commit: commit });
  }).then(function (r) {
    $("start").disabled = false;
    if (!r.ok) { $("status").textContent = r.body.error || "That didn't work."; return; }
    gameId = r.body.game;
    mine = { seat: r.body.seat, key: r.body.key, board: draft.board, salt: draft.salt };
    save(gameId, mine);
    history.replaceState(null, "", "#" + gameId);
    refresh();
  });
};

makeGrid("left");
makeGrid("right", fire);
$("shuffle").onclick = function () { draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }; render(); };
$("start").onclick = begin;
$("claim").onclick = claim;
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = function (e) {
  var g = JSON.parse(e.data);
  if (g.id === gameId && (mine || g.phase !== "waiting")) { game = g; render(); }
};
if (gameId) { mine = load(gameId); refresh(); } else render();

<script>
var FLEET = [4, 3, 3, 2];
var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
var memory = {};
var load = function (id) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("sea-battle:" + id)) || memory[id] || null; } catch (e) { return memory[id] || null; } };
var save = function (id, v) { memory[id] = v; try { localStorage.setItem("sea-battle:" + id, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} };

var hex = function (bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, function (b) { return (b + 256).toString(16).slice(1); }).join(""); };
var sha = function (text) {
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text)).then(function (d) { return hex(new Uint8Array(d)); });
  return Promise.resolve(own(text));
};
// sha256 for a page served over plain http on a network, where the browser offers none.
var own = function (text) {
  var bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text), primes = [];
  for (var c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every(function (p) { return c % p; })) primes.push(c);
  var frac = function (x) { return x - Math.floor(x); };
  var K = primes.map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var h = primes.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6, buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes); buf[bytes.length] = 128;
  var view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912)); view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  var w = new Array(64), rotr = function (x, n) { return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n)); };
  for (var off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (var i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++) w[i] = (w[i - 16] + (rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)) + w[i - 7] + (rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10))) >>> 0;
    var a = h[0], b = h[1], cc = h[2], d = h[3], e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      var t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      var t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = cc; cc = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, cc, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach(function (v, i) { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map(function (v) { return (v + 4294967296).toString(16).slice(1); }).join("");
};

// A fleet placed at random: ships 1 to 4, each a straight line, none overlapping.
var shuffle = function () {
  var cells = new Array(64).fill("0");
  FLEET.forEach(function (len, i) {
    for (;;) {
      var across = Math.random() < 0.5;
      var r = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 8 : 9 - len)), c = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 9 - len : 8));
      var at = [];
      for (var k = 0; k < len; k++) at.push((r + (across ? 0 : k)) * 8 + c + (across ? k : 0));
      if (at.every(function (x) { return cells[x] === "0"; })) { at.forEach(function (x) { cells[x] = String(i + 1); }); return; }
    }
  });
  return cells.join("");
};
var salt = function () { var b = new Uint8Array(16); crypto.getRandomValues(b); return hex(b); };

var grids = {};
var makeGrid = function (id, onClick) {
  var el = $(id), cells = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < 64; k++) {
    var btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label", "ABCDEFGH"[k % 8] + (Math.floor(k / 8) + 1));
    (function (k) { btn.onclick = function () { if (onClick) onClick(k); }; })(k);
    el.appendChild(btn); cells.push(btn);
  }
  grids[id] = cells;
};
var paint = function (id, cls) { grids[id].forEach(function (b, k) { b.className = cls(k); }); };

var post = function (path, body) {
  return fetch(path, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })
    .then(function (r) { return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (j) { return { ok: r.ok, body: j }; }); });
};

var gameId = location.hash.slice(1), game = null, mine = null, draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }, busy = false;

var marks = function (shots, k) {
  var s = shots.find(function (s) { return s.cell === k; });
  return s ? (s.said === "" ? " wait" : " " + s.said) : "";
};
var render = function () {
  var g = game;
  if (!g) {
    paint("left", function (k) { return draft.board[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : ""; });
    paint("right", function () { return ""; });
    $("leftname").textContent = "Your fleet (only you can see it)";
    $("rightname").textContent = "Their waters";
    return;
  }

  var left = me || "a", right = me ? them : "b";
  var atLeft = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by !== left; });
  var atRight = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === left; });
  var boardOf = function (seat) { return seat === me ? mine.board : g[seat].board; };
  var lies = function (seat) { return g[seat].lies || []; };
  var cls = function (seat, shots) {
    return function (k) {
      var b = boardOf(seat);
      return (b && b[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : "") + marks(shots, k) + (lies(seat).indexOf(k) >= 0 ? " lie" : "");
    };
  };

  paint("right", cls(right, atRight));
  $("leftname").textContent = (me ? "Your waters" : (g.a.name || "Captain A") + "'s waters");

  $("leftseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[left].commit || "...");

<main>
<h1>Sea battle</h1>
<p class="dim">The server never sees your fleet. It keeps a seal of it, writes down every answer you give, and checks them all when the boards are revealed.</p>

<section id="lobby">
<div class="bar"><input type="text" id="name" placeholder="Your name" maxlength="24"><button class="go quiet" id="shuffle">Shuffle fleet</button><button class="go" id="start">Start a game</button></div>
</section>

<p id="status"></p>
<p id="share" style="display:none">Send this link to your opponent: <span id="link"></span></p>
<div class="bar" id="cheat" style="display:none"><label><input type="checkbox" id="lie"> Lie about hits (answer "miss" when they hit me)</label></div>

<div class="seas">
<div class="sea"><h2 id="leftname">Your waters</h2><div class="grid" id="left"></div><p class="seal" id="leftseal"></p></div>
<div class="sea"><h2 id="rightname">Their waters</h2><div class="grid" id="right"></div><p class="seal" id="rightseal"></p></div>
</div>

<script>
var FLEET = [4, 3, 3, 2];
var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
var memory = {};
var load = function (id) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("sea-battle:" + id)) || memory[id] || null; } catch (e) { return memory[id] || null; } };
var save = function (id, v) { memory[id] = v; try { localStorage.setItem("sea-battle:" + id, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} };

var hex = function (bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, function (b) { return (b + 256).toString(16).slice(1); }).join(""); };
var sha = function (text) {
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text)).then(function (d) { return hex(new Uint8Array(d)); });
  return Promise.resolve(own(text));
};
// sha256 for a page served over plain http on a network, where the browser offers none.
var own = function (text) {
  var bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text), primes = [];
  for (var c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every(function (p) { return c % p; })) primes.push(c);
  var frac = function (x) { return x - Math.floor(x); };
  var K = primes.map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var h = primes.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6, buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes); buf[bytes.length] = 128;
  var view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912)); view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  var w = new Array(64), rotr = function (x, n) { return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n)); };
  for (var off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (var i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++) w[i] = (w[i - 16] + (rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)) + w[i - 7] + (rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10))) >>> 0;
    var a = h[0], b = h[1], cc = h[2], d = h[3], e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      var t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      var t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = cc; cc = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, cc, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach(function (v, i) { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map(function (v) { return (v + 4294967296).toString(16).slice(1); }).join("");
};

// A fleet placed at random: ships 1 to 4, each a straight line, none overlapping.
var shuffle = function () {
  var cells = new Array(64).fill("0");
  FLEET.forEach(function (len, i) {
    for (;;) {
      var across = Math.random() < 0.5;
      var r = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 8 : 9 - len)), c = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 9 - len : 8));
      var at = [];
      for (var k = 0; k < len; k++) at.push((r + (across ? 0 : k)) * 8 + c + (across ? k : 0));
      if (at.every(function (x) { return cells[x] === "0"; })) { at.forEach(function (x) { cells[x] = String(i + 1); }); return; }
    }
  });
  return cells.join("");
};
var salt = function () { var b = new Uint8Array(16); crypto.getRandomValues(b); return hex(b); };

var grids = {};
var makeGrid = function (id, onClick) {
  var el = $(id), cells = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < 64; k++) {
    var btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label", "ABCDEFGH"[k % 8] + (Math.floor(k / 8) + 1));
    (function (k) { btn.onclick = function () { if (onClick) onClick(k); }; })(k);
    el.appendChild(btn); cells.push(btn);
  }
  grids[id] = cells;
};
var paint = function (id, cls) { grids[id].forEach(function (b, k) { b.className = cls(k); }); };

var post = function (path, body) {
  return fetch(path, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })
    .then(function (r) { return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (j) { return { ok: r.ok, body: j }; }); });
};

var gameId = location.hash.slice(1), game = null, mine = null, draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }, busy = false;

var marks = function (shots, k) {
  var s = shots.find(function (s) { return s.cell === k; });
  return s ? (s.said === "" ? " wait" : " " + s.said) : "";
};
var render = function () {
  var g = game;
  if (!g) {
    paint("left", function (k) { return draft.board[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : ""; });
    paint("right", function () { return ""; });
    $("leftname").textContent = "Your fleet (only you can see it)";
    $("rightname").textContent = "Their waters";
    return;
  }
  var me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var left = me || "a", right = me ? them : "b";
  var atLeft = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by !== left; });
  var atRight = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === left; });
  var boardOf = function (seat) { return seat === me ? mine.board : g[seat].board; };
  var lies = function (seat) { return g[seat].lies || []; };
  var cls = function (seat, shots) {
    return function (k) {
      var b = boardOf(seat);
      return (b && b[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : "") + marks(shots, k) + (lies(seat).indexOf(k) >= 0 ? " lie" : "");
    };
  };
  paint("left", cls(left, atLeft));
  paint("right", cls(right, atRight));
  $("leftname").textContent = (me ? "Your waters" : (g.a.name || "Captain A") + "'s waters");
  $("rightname").textContent = (g[right].name || "Waiting for a captain") + "'s waters";
  $("leftseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[left].commit || "...");
  $("rightseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[right].commit || "...");
  var aim = me && g.phase === "play" && g.turn === me && !g.shots.some(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  $("right").className = "grid" + (aim ? " aim" : "");
  $("lobby").style.display = "none";
  $("share").style.display = me === "a" && g.phase === "waiting" ? "" : "none";
  $("link").textContent = location.href;
  $("cheat").style.display = me && (g.phase === "play" || g.phase === "waiting") ? "" : "none";
  var hits = function (seat) { return g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === seat && s.said === "hit"; }).length; };
  var say = g.phase === "waiting" ? (me ? "Waiting for an opponent to join." : "This game is waiting for a second captain.")
    : g.phase === "play" ? (me ? (g.turn === me ? "Your turn: fire at their waters." : "Their turn.") : g[g.turn].name + " is firing.") + " Hits " + hits(left) + " / 12, taken " + hits(right) + " / 12."
    : g.phase === "reveal" ? "The battle is over. The boards are being revealed and checked."
    : g.winner ? g[g.winner].name + " wins." : "Nobody wins.";
  $("status").textContent = say;
  var v = $("verdict");
  if (g.phase === "over") {
    var line = function (seat) {
      var p = g[seat];
      if (!p.revealed) return "<p><b></b>: <span class=bad>went silent and never revealed a board</span></p>";
      return "<p><b></b>: " + (p.sealed ? "<span class=ok>board matches its seal</span>" : "<span class=bad>board does not match its seal</span>")
        + ", " + (p.fleet === "" ? "<span class=ok>legal fleet</span>" : "<span class=bad>illegal fleet</span>")
        + ", " + (p.lies.length ? "<span class=bad>" + p.lies.length + " false answers (marked in yellow)</span>" : "<span class=ok>every answer true</span>") + "</p>";
    };
    v.innerHTML = "<p id=why></p>" + line("a") + line("b");
    v.querySelector("#why").textContent = g.why;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[0].textContent = g.a.name;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[1].textContent = g.b.name;
    v.className = "on";
  } else v.className = "";
  silence();
  act();
};

// What this page does by itself: answer a shot at it, and reveal its board when the battle ends.
var act = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy) return;
  var waiting = g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === "" && s.by !== me; });
  if (g.phase === "play" && waiting) {
    var hit = mine.board[waiting.cell] !== "0";
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/answer", { key: mine.key, said: hit && !$("lie").checked ? "hit" : "miss" }).then(done);
  } else if (g.phase === "reveal" && !g[me].revealed) {
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/reveal", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
  }
};
var done = function (r) { busy = false; if (r.ok && r.body.id) { game = r.body; render(); } };

// When the opponent has kept everyone waiting two minutes, a button to claim the game.
var silence = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var pending = g && g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  var waitingOn = !g ? "" : g.phase === "reveal" ? (g[them].revealed ? "" : them) : g.phase !== "play" ? "" : pending ? (pending.by === me ? them : me) : g.turn;
  var left = g ? 120 - (Date.now() / 1000 - g.since) : 0;
  var can = me && waitingOn === them && (g.phase === "play" || g[me].revealed) && left <= 0;
  $("claimbar").style.display = can ? "" : "none";
  if (can) $("claim").textContent = g[them].name + " has gone silent. Claim the game";
};
setInterval(function () { if (game) silence(); }, 5000);
var claim = function () {
  if (busy) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/claim", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
};

var fire = function (k) {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy || g.phase !== "play" || g.turn !== me) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/fire", { key: mine.key, cell: k }).then(done);
};

var refresh = function () {
  return fetch("/games/" + gameId).then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; }).then(function (g) {
    game = g;
    if (!g) { $("status").textContent = "There is no game " + gameId + "."; return; }
    if (!mine && g.phase === "waiting") {
      $("start").textContent = "Join " + (g.a.name || "this game");
      $("lobby").style.display = "";
      $("status").textContent = (g.a.name || "A captain") + " is waiting for an opponent. Shuffle your fleet and join.";
      game = null; render(); game = g;
      return;
    }
    render();
  });
};

var begin = function () {
  var name = $("name").value.trim();
  $("start").disabled = true;
  sha(draft.board + "|" + draft.salt).then(function (commit) {
    return post(gameId ? "/games/" + gameId + "/join" : "/games", { name: name || undefined, commit: commit });
  }).then(function (r) {
    $("start").disabled = false;
    if (!r.ok) { $("status").textContent = r.body.error || "That didn't work."; return; }
    gameId = r.body.game;
    mine = { seat: r.body.seat, key: r.body.key, board: draft.board, salt: draft.salt };
    save(gameId, mine);
    history.replaceState(null, "", "#" + gameId);
    refresh();
  });
};

makeGrid("left");
makeGrid("right", fire);
$("shuffle").onclick = function () { draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }; render(); };
$("start").onclick = begin;
$("claim").onclick = claim;
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = function (e) {
  var g = JSON.parse(e.data);
  if (g.id === gameId && (mine || g.phase !== "waiting")) { game = g; render(); }
};
if (gameId) { mine = load(gameId); refresh(); } else render();
</script>

<script>
var FLEET = [4, 3, 3, 2];
var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
var memory = {};
var load = function (id) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("sea-battle:" + id)) || memory[id] || null; } catch (e) { return memory[id] || null; } };
var save = function (id, v) { memory[id] = v; try { localStorage.setItem("sea-battle:" + id, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} };

var hex = function (bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, function (b) { return (b + 256).toString(16).slice(1); }).join(""); };
var sha = function (text) {
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text)).then(function (d) { return hex(new Uint8Array(d)); });
  return Promise.resolve(own(text));
};
// sha256 for a page served over plain http on a network, where the browser offers none.
var own = function (text) {
  var bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text), primes = [];
  for (var c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every(function (p) { return c % p; })) primes.push(c);
  var frac = function (x) { return x - Math.floor(x); };
  var K = primes.map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var h = primes.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6, buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes); buf[bytes.length] = 128;
  var view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912)); view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  var w = new Array(64), rotr = function (x, n) { return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n)); };
  for (var off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (var i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++) w[i] = (w[i - 16] + (rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)) + w[i - 7] + (rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10))) >>> 0;
    var a = h[0], b = h[1], cc = h[2], d = h[3], e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      var t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      var t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = cc; cc = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, cc, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach(function (v, i) { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map(function (v) { return (v + 4294967296).toString(16).slice(1); }).join("");
};

// A fleet placed at random: ships 1 to 4, each a straight line, none overlapping.
var shuffle = function () {
  var cells = new Array(64).fill("0");
  FLEET.forEach(function (len, i) {
    for (;;) {
      var across = Math.random() < 0.5;
      var r = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 8 : 9 - len)), c = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 9 - len : 8));
      var at = [];
      for (var k = 0; k < len; k++) at.push((r + (across ? 0 : k)) * 8 + c + (across ? k : 0));
      if (at.every(function (x) { return cells[x] === "0"; })) { at.forEach(function (x) { cells[x] = String(i + 1); }); return; }
    }
  });
  return cells.join("");
};
var salt = function () { var b = new Uint8Array(16); crypto.getRandomValues(b); return hex(b); };

var grids = {};
var makeGrid = function (id, onClick) {
  var el = $(id), cells = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < 64; k++) {
    var btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label", "ABCDEFGH"[k % 8] + (Math.floor(k / 8) + 1));
    (function (k) { btn.onclick = function () { if (onClick) onClick(k); }; })(k);
    el.appendChild(btn); cells.push(btn);
  }
  grids[id] = cells;
};
var paint = function (id, cls) { grids[id].forEach(function (b, k) { b.className = cls(k); }); };

var post = function (path, body) {
  return fetch(path, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })
    .then(function (r) { return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (j) { return { ok: r.ok, body: j }; }); });
};

var gameId = location.hash.slice(1), game = null, mine = null, draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }, busy = false;

var marks = function (shots, k) {
  var s = shots.find(function (s) { return s.cell === k; });
  return s ? (s.said === "" ? " wait" : " " + s.said) : "";
};
var render = function () {
  var g = game;
  if (!g) {
    paint("left", function (k) { return draft.board[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : ""; });
    paint("right", function () { return ""; });
    $("leftname").textContent = "Your fleet (only you can see it)";
    $("rightname").textContent = "Their waters";
    return;
  }
  var me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var left = me || "a", right = me ? them : "b";
  var atLeft = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by !== left; });
  var atRight = g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === left; });
  var boardOf = function (seat) { return seat === me ? mine.board : g[seat].board; };
  var lies = function (seat) { return g[seat].lies || []; };
  var cls = function (seat, shots) {
    return function (k) {
      var b = boardOf(seat);
      return (b && b[k] !== "0" ? "ship" : "") + marks(shots, k) + (lies(seat).indexOf(k) >= 0 ? " lie" : "");
    };
  };
  paint("left", cls(left, atLeft));
  paint("right", cls(right, atRight));
  $("leftname").textContent = (me ? "Your waters" : (g.a.name || "Captain A") + "'s waters");
  $("rightname").textContent = (g[right].name || "Waiting for a captain") + "'s waters";
  $("leftseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[left].commit || "...");
  $("rightseal").textContent = "seal " + (g[right].commit || "...");
  var aim = me && g.phase === "play" && g.turn === me && !g.shots.some(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  $("right").className = "grid" + (aim ? " aim" : "");
  $("lobby").style.display = "none";
  $("share").style.display = me === "a" && g.phase === "waiting" ? "" : "none";
  $("link").textContent = location.href;
  $("cheat").style.display = me && (g.phase === "play" || g.phase === "waiting") ? "" : "none";
  var hits = function (seat) { return g.shots.filter(function (s) { return s.by === seat && s.said === "hit"; }).length; };
  var say = g.phase === "waiting" ? (me ? "Waiting for an opponent to join." : "This game is waiting for a second captain.")
    : g.phase === "play" ? (me ? (g.turn === me ? "Your turn: fire at their waters." : "Their turn.") : g[g.turn].name + " is firing.") + " Hits " + hits(left) + " / 12, taken " + hits(right) + " / 12."
    : g.phase === "reveal" ? "The battle is over. The boards are being revealed and checked."
    : g.winner ? g[g.winner].name + " wins." : "Nobody wins.";
  $("status").textContent = say;
  var v = $("verdict");
  if (g.phase === "over") {
    var line = function (seat) {
      var p = g[seat];
      if (!p.revealed) return "<p><b></b>: <span class=bad>went silent and never revealed a board</span></p>";
      return "<p><b></b>: " + (p.sealed ? "<span class=ok>board matches its seal</span>" : "<span class=bad>board does not match its seal</span>")
        + ", " + (p.fleet === "" ? "<span class=ok>legal fleet</span>" : "<span class=bad>illegal fleet</span>")
        + ", " + (p.lies.length ? "<span class=bad>" + p.lies.length + " false answers (marked in yellow)</span>" : "<span class=ok>every answer true</span>") + "</p>";
    };
    v.innerHTML = "<p id=why></p>" + line("a") + line("b");
    v.querySelector("#why").textContent = g.why;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[0].textContent = g.a.name;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[1].textContent = g.b.name;
    v.className = "on";
  } else v.className = "";
  silence();
  act();
};

// What this page does by itself: answer a shot at it, and reveal its board when the battle ends.
var act = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy) return;
  var waiting = g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === "" && s.by !== me; });
  if (g.phase === "play" && waiting) {
    var hit = mine.board[waiting.cell] !== "0";
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/answer", { key: mine.key, said: hit && !$("lie").checked ? "hit" : "miss" }).then(done);
  } else if (g.phase === "reveal" && !g[me].revealed) {
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/reveal", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
  }
};
var done = function (r) { busy = false; if (r.ok && r.body.id) { game = r.body; render(); } };

// When the opponent has kept everyone waiting two minutes, a button to claim the game.
var silence = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var pending = g && g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  var waitingOn = !g ? "" : g.phase === "reveal" ? (g[them].revealed ? "" : them) : g.phase !== "play" ? "" : pending ? (pending.by === me ? them : me) : g.turn;
  var left = g ? 120 - (Date.now() / 1000 - g.since) : 0;
  var can = me && waitingOn === them && (g.phase === "play" || g[me].revealed) && left <= 0;
  $("claimbar").style.display = can ? "" : "none";
  if (can) $("claim").textContent = g[them].name + " has gone silent. Claim the game";
};
setInterval(function () { if (game) silence(); }, 5000);
var claim = function () {
  if (busy) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/claim", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
};

var fire = function (k) {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy || g.phase !== "play" || g.turn !== me) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/fire", { key: mine.key, cell: k }).then(done);
};

var refresh = function () {
  return fetch("/games/" + gameId).then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; }).then(function (g) {
    game = g;
    if (!g) { $("status").textContent = "There is no game " + gameId + "."; return; }
    if (!mine && g.phase === "waiting") {
      $("start").textContent = "Join " + (g.a.name || "this game");
      $("lobby").style.display = "";
      $("status").textContent = (g.a.name || "A captain") + " is waiting for an opponent. Shuffle your fleet and join.";
      game = null; render(); game = g;
      return;
    }
    render();
  });
};

var begin = function () {
  var name = $("name").value.trim();
  $("start").disabled = true;
  sha(draft.board + "|" + draft.salt).then(function (commit) {
    return post(gameId ? "/games/" + gameId + "/join" : "/games", { name: name || undefined, commit: commit });
  }).then(function (r) {
    $("start").disabled = false;
    if (!r.ok) { $("status").textContent = r.body.error || "That didn't work."; return; }
    gameId = r.body.game;
    mine = { seat: r.body.seat, key: r.body.key, board: draft.board, salt: draft.salt };
    save(gameId, mine);
    history.replaceState(null, "", "#" + gameId);
    refresh();
  });
};

makeGrid("left");
makeGrid("right", fire);
$("shuffle").onclick = function () { draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }; render(); };
$("start").onclick = begin;
$("claim").onclick = claim;
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = function (e) {
  var g = JSON.parse(e.data);
  if (g.id === gameId && (mine || g.phase !== "waiting")) { game = g; render(); }
};
if (gameId) { mine = load(gameId); refresh(); } else render();

<script>
var FLEET = [4, 3, 3, 2];
var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
var memory = {};
var load = function (id) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("sea-battle:" + id)) || memory[id] || null; } catch (e) { return memory[id] || null; } };
var save = function (id, v) { memory[id] = v; try { localStorage.setItem("sea-battle:" + id, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} };

var hex = function (bytes) { return Array.from(bytes, function (b) { return (b + 256).toString(16).slice(1); }).join(""); };
var sha = function (text) {
  if (window.crypto && crypto.subtle) return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(text)).then(function (d) { return hex(new Uint8Array(d)); });
  return Promise.resolve(own(text));
};
// sha256 for a page served over plain http on a network, where the browser offers none.
var own = function (text) {
  var bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text), primes = [];
  for (var c = 2; primes.length < 64; c++) if (primes.every(function (p) { return c % p; })) primes.push(c);
  var frac = function (x) { return x - Math.floor(x); };
  var K = primes.map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.cbrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var h = primes.slice(0, 8).map(function (p) { return Math.floor(frac(Math.sqrt(p)) * 4294967296); });
  var size = ((bytes.length + 9 + 63) >> 6) << 6, buf = new Uint8Array(size);
  buf.set(bytes); buf[bytes.length] = 128;
  var view = new DataView(buf.buffer);
  view.setUint32(size - 8, Math.floor(bytes.length / 536870912)); view.setUint32(size - 4, (bytes.length << 3) >>> 0);
  var w = new Array(64), rotr = function (x, n) { return (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n)); };
  for (var off = 0; off < size; off += 64) {
    for (var i = 0; i < 16; i++) w[i] = view.getUint32(off + i * 4);
    for (i = 16; i < 64; i++) w[i] = (w[i - 16] + (rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >>> 3)) + w[i - 7] + (rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >>> 10))) >>> 0;
    var a = h[0], b = h[1], cc = h[2], d = h[3], e = h[4], f = h[5], g = h[6], hh = h[7];
    for (i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
      var t1 = (hh + (rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25)) + ((e & f) ^ (~e & g)) + K[i] + w[i]) >>> 0;
      var t2 = ((rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22)) + ((a & b) ^ (a & cc) ^ (b & cc))) >>> 0;
      hh = g; g = f; f = e; e = (d + t1) >>> 0; d = cc; cc = b; b = a; a = (t1 + t2) >>> 0;
    }
    [a, b, cc, d, e, f, g, hh].forEach(function (v, i) { h[i] = (h[i] + v) >>> 0; });
  }
  return h.map(function (v) { return (v + 4294967296).toString(16).slice(1); }).join("");
};

// A fleet placed at random: ships 1 to 4, each a straight line, none overlapping.
var shuffle = function () {
  var cells = new Array(64).fill("0");
  FLEET.forEach(function (len, i) {
    for (;;) {
      var across = Math.random() < 0.5;
      var r = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 8 : 9 - len)), c = Math.floor(Math.random() * (across ? 9 - len : 8));
      var at = [];
      for (var k = 0; k < len; k++) at.push((r + (across ? 0 : k)) * 8 + c + (across ? k : 0));
      if (at.every(function (x) { return cells[x] === "0"; })) { at.forEach(function (x) { cells[x] = String(i + 1); }); return; }
    }
  });
  return cells.join("");
};

  }

};

        + ", " + (p.lies.length ? "<span class=bad>" + p.lies.length + " false answers (marked in yellow)</span>" : "<span class=ok>every answer true</span>") + "</p>";
    };
    v.innerHTML = "<p id=why></p>" + line("a") + line("b");

    v.querySelectorAll("b")[0].textContent = g.a.name;
    v.querySelectorAll("b")[1].textContent = g.b.name;
    v.className = "on";
  } else v.className = "";
  silence();
  act();
};

// What this page does by itself: answer a shot at it, and reveal its board when the battle ends.
var act = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy) return;
  var waiting = g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === "" && s.by !== me; });
  if (g.phase === "play" && waiting) {
    var hit = mine.board[waiting.cell] !== "0";
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/answer", { key: mine.key, said: hit && !$("lie").checked ? "hit" : "miss" }).then(done);
  } else if (g.phase === "reveal" && !g[me].revealed) {
    busy = true;
    post("/games/" + gameId + "/reveal", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
  }
};
var done = function (r) { busy = false; if (r.ok && r.body.id) { game = r.body; render(); } };

// When the opponent has kept everyone waiting two minutes, a button to claim the game.
var silence = function () {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat, them = me === "b" ? "a" : "b";
  var pending = g && g.shots.find(function (s) { return s.said === ""; });
  var waitingOn = !g ? "" : g.phase === "reveal" ? (g[them].revealed ? "" : them) : g.phase !== "play" ? "" : pending ? (pending.by === me ? them : me) : g.turn;
  var left = g ? 120 - (Date.now() / 1000 - g.since) : 0;
  var can = me && waitingOn === them && (g.phase === "play" || g[me].revealed) && left <= 0;
  $("claimbar").style.display = can ? "" : "none";
  if (can) $("claim").textContent = g[them].name + " has gone silent. Claim the game";
};
setInterval(function () { if (game) silence(); }, 5000);
var claim = function () {
  if (busy) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/claim", { key: mine.key, board: mine.board, salt: mine.salt }).then(done);
};

var fire = function (k) {
  var g = game, me = mine && mine.seat;
  if (!g || !me || busy || g.phase !== "play" || g.turn !== me) return;
  busy = true;
  post("/games/" + gameId + "/fire", { key: mine.key, cell: k }).then(done);
};

var refresh = function () {
  return fetch("/games/" + gameId).then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; }).then(function (g) {
    game = g;
    if (!g) { $("status").textContent = "There is no game " + gameId + "."; return; }
    if (!mine && g.phase === "waiting") {
      $("start").textContent = "Join " + (g.a.name || "this game");
      $("lobby").style.display = "";
      $("status").textContent = (g.a.name || "A captain") + " is waiting for an opponent. Shuffle your fleet and join.";
      game = null; render(); game = g;
      return;
    }
    render();
  });
};

var begin = function () {
  var name = $("name").value.trim();
  $("start").disabled = true;
  sha(draft.board + "|" + draft.salt).then(function (commit) {
    return post(gameId ? "/games/" + gameId + "/join" : "/games", { name: name || undefined, commit: commit });
  }).then(function (r) {
    $("start").disabled = false;
    if (!r.ok) { $("status").textContent = r.body.error || "That didn't work."; return; }
    gameId = r.body.game;
    mine = { seat: r.body.seat, key: r.body.key, board: draft.board, salt: draft.salt };
    save(gameId, mine);
    history.replaceState(null, "", "#" + gameId);
    refresh();
  });
};

makeGrid("left");
makeGrid("right", fire);
$("shuffle").onclick = function () { draft = { board: shuffle(), salt: salt() }; render(); };
$("start").onclick = begin;
$("claim").onclick = claim;
new EventSource("/live").onmessage = function (e) {
  var g = JSON.parse(e.data);
  if (g.id === gameId && (mine || g.phase !== "waiting")) { game = g; render(); }
};
if (gameId) { mine = load(gameId); refresh(); } else render();
